<!-- page 1 -->
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
Tuesday, October 14, 2014 
 
PRESENT:  Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor; Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor; David Tenney, Supervisor; and 
Jesse Thompson, Chairman were all present in person.  
9:02 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Supervisor Tenney.  
CALL TO THE PUBLIC : Melissa Buckley, Clerk of the Board presented a certificate of appreciation to 
Navajo County for their support  to Navajo Mountain and Surrounding Communities for the 49 th Annual 
Fehaniih Day Celebration. Mrs. Buckley also read a letter  from White Mountain Community Garden thanking 
Navajo County Manager, James Jayne, County Engineer, Bill Bess, County Building Director, Bret Starns and 
County Building Inspector, Rob Douglas for their support with the Community Garden. 
CONSENT AGENDA:  a) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000, September 2014 b) Minutes: September 9, 
2014; September 12, 2014 Special Meeting; September 23, 2014; September 30, 2014 Special Meeting 
c) Justice Court Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, September; Winslow Precinct #2, September; Snowflake 
Precinct #3, September; Kaye nta Precinct #4, September; Show Low Precinct #5, September; and Pinetop -
Lakeside Precinct #6, September, 2014 d) constable Reports: Winslow Precinct #2, August and September 2014 
e) Juvenile Probation Report,  September 2014  f) Clerk of the Superior Court Report, September 2014 g) 
Contract signed by County Manager per Board of Supervisors Resolution: Contract for Professional Services 
Agreement with Roman Bitsuie h) Authorization for Public Works to increase the contingency funding an 
additional $50,000 for the Public Works Complex contract, #B13 -06-021 with Haydon Building Corporation i) 
Task Agreement Number 15 to the Memorandum of Agreement between the Bureau of Indian Affairs (BIA), 
Fort Apache Agency and Navajo County to fund labor and parts to repair a n IT28 Loader j) Task Agreement 
Number 16 to the Memorandum of Agreement between the Bureau of Indian Affairs (BIA), Fort Apache 
Agency and Navajo County for the purchase of labor, equipment and materials for the pavement striping of 14.6 
miles of BIA Route 12 at Cibecu, AZ k) Letter of congratulations to Chairman Honaine for the successful bit of 
the Tiger Grant l) Letter of appreciation to Senator Bob Worsley : Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to 
authorize the C hairman to sign the items in the consent agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
 HUMAN RESOURCES: Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions:  Eric Scott reviewed the 
16 item personnel  action report. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the personnel actions as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
 
COUNTY ATTORNEY: Consideration and possible approval to authorize the Navajo Coun ty Attorney’s 
Office to hire an outside attorney on behalf of Pinal County to handle a conflict criminal case: County Attorney, 
Brad Carlyon shared with the board details of the contract with Pinal County to hire an outside attorney .  
Supervisor Whitesinger  asked for clarification on the payment  and Mr. Carlyon confirmed  Navajo County 
would be reviewing the bills for accuracy and submits to Pinal County for payment. Supervisor Whitesinger 
made a motion to approve the authorization of Navajo County Attorney’s Office to hire an outside attorney on 
behalf of Pinal County to handle a conflict case ; motion seconded by  Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 10-14-2014 
    Page 2 of 4 
 
EMERGENCY MANAGEMENT: Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 32-14, 
approving and authorizing the Chairman to execute the revised Arizona Mutual Aid Compact:  Catrina Roe 
clarified revisions of the Resolution Number 32-14. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve and adopt 
Resolution Number 32-14 approving and authorizing the Chairman to execute the revised Arizona Mutual Aid 
Compact; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
 
ELECTIONS:  
a) Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 33-14, approving designated polling places, 
pursuant to A.R.S. § 16-411(B): Johnathan Roes commented on the Resolution approving polling places.  
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to adopt Resolution Number 33-14 approving designated polling 
places pursuant to A.R.S. § 16-411(B); motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion. 
b) Consideration and possible approval of the Poll Workers for the General Election: Johnathan Roes 
commented on the item of approving the poll workers for the general election.  Supervisor Tenney made a 
motion to approve designated poll workers for the general election; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
 
PUBLIC WORKS: PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 34 
14, approving a Special Use Permit for a Meteorological Tower, not to exceed 196 feet in height, located on 
Assessor’s Parcel Number 110-05-001D: Mr. Vela presented details of the resolution with recommendations to 
approve the special use permit being retroactive to installment date for a Meteorological Tower. Supervisor 
Tenney questioned why the owners did not submit for a special use permit when the tower was installed.  Mrs. 
Saunders responded because most of their business has been done in Texas and Texas does not require permits. 
Chairman Thompson opened the public hearing, there being no comments, closed the public hearing. 
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to adopt Resolution Number 34-14 approving the Special Use Permit for 
a Meteorological Tower, not to exceed 196 feet in height, located on Assessor’s Parcel Number 110-05-001D; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
 
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager: 
Supervisor Nez reported on the activity happening with road materials being placed within District I.  He 
thanked BIA, Navajo Nation, Homero Vela and his team for the partnership for road improvement work. 
Supervisor Nez commented that he would be attending the National Indian Education Association conference 
in Alaska. Supervisor Nez updated everyone on the issues with the Navajo Nation Presidential Election. 
Supervisor Whitesinger reported on attending the Good Neighbors meeting and how grateful she was to be a 
part of the conversation on building good communities and tackling the issues of substance abuse and 
depression in the White Mountain Apache Tribe.  She invited others to participate in a Summit on November 
12, 2014 at Hondah if anyone is interested in attending and being a part of the discussion.  She gave the 
welcome address to the Tour of the White Mountains and welcomes the 700 mountain bike participants.  
Supervisor Whitesinger talked about the excellent turnout they received on the junior pass promotion day at 
Sunrise Ski Resort.  She expressed thanks to the Board, Homero Vela, Bill Bess and all who assisted in 
supporting the striping of BIA road 12.  Supervisor Tenney updated the Board on his trip to Malta MT. for the 
NACo, Western Interstate Region (WIR) conference.  He indicated about 40 participants were on hand to 
discuss public land issues; PILT and how to get congress to permanently fund PILT.  We discussed other issues 
including endangered species and sage grass.  Supervisor Tenney discussed touring some the ongoing projects 
in the White Mountains, of the Apache Sitgraves Forest and helping the Pinedale Cemetery work on transfer of 
ownership with the US Forest Service who has owned the property for 140 years.  He also discussed attending

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 10-14-2014 
    Page 3 of 4 
 
the CSA Summit in Prescott and what a great opportunity to work with other County Supervisors and put 
together a list of legislative priorities.  One big issue is the State budget and how that is going to affect the 
Counties.  County Manager James Jayne commented on the upcoming 2014 County Leadership Academy 
graduation on, Wednesday October 29th at 9:00am in the George Gardner Performing Arts center in Holbrook.   
This is the 2nd graduating class and invited the Board members to attend. He thanked Melissa for her leadership 
in coordinating this endeavor. County Attorney, Brad Carlyon commented he has been with AACo staff visiting 
counties for the Annual Business for Counties throughout Arizona.  He discussed the annual banquet fundraiser 
for the Bread of Life mission who is a great partner with Navajo County for assisting people in the community 
who need assistance with sleeping quarters and other items.  Mr. Carlyon and Navajo County Sheriff, KC Clark 
with Community Bridges are working on a new prescription drug program.  Through this education outreach 
program, we are not only educating our citizens but our medical community as well.  Chairman Thompson 
expressed thanks to the Navajo County Recorder’s office & Elections staff for the support with early voting; 
there was good turnout in Hopi and Dilkon.  He mentioned that Cibeque is interested in the early voting so in 
the future we would like to assist them with  
 
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS: Consideration and possible approval of the list of candidates to be interviewed: 
Supervisor Tenney discussed the item outlining the candidates to be interviewed, Jason E. Whiting, Bill 
Elkins, David Smith, Joel E. Lawson Jr, Paul Hatch, Hunter Lewis and Thomas Poscharsky. Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to approve the list of candidates to be interviewed; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Chairman Thompson asked Supervisor Tenney to 
restate the process. Supervisor Tenney outlined the process and thanked all 20 applicants for going through 
this process.  
 
EXECUTIVE SESSION pursuant to A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (1) regarding interviews for District III Supervisor 
vacancy: At 9:57a.m. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to enter into Executive Session; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
Interviews conducted of the following candidates: Jason E. Whiting, Bill Elkins, David Smith, Joel E. 
Lawson Jr, Paul Hatch, Hunter Lewis, and Thomas Poscharsky.  
 
RECONVENE for action related to the appointment of District III Supervisor: At 2:00 p.m. Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion. Chairman Thompson expressed his thanks to each and every one of the candidates for 
being willing to be a part of this process. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to appoint Mr. Jason E. Whiting 
as District III Supervisor; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion. Congratulations Jason Whiting. Mr. Jason Whiting addressed the Board, thanked the board for their 
consideration and for all those that applied.  He indicated that his heart is in serving District III.  
 
ADJOURN: At 2:11 p.m. Supervisor Nez made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.
